### `Scope_Dynamic_Flow/Scope_Notifications/Compose_Notify_Config`

| Field | As provisioned |
|---|---|
| Type | `Compose` |
| Kind | — |
| Description | _not declared_ |
| Runs after | `Compose_Notify_Logo_Default` (Succeeded) |

**`inputs`, verbatim:**

```json
{
  "enabled": "@not(equals(toLower(trim(string(xpath(outputs('Compose_Notify_Config_Xml'),'string(/r/i[k=\"NOTIFY_ENABLED\"]/v)')))),'false'))",
  "opsTo": "@if(empty(trim(string(xpath(outputs('Compose_Notify_Config_Xml'),'string(/r/i[k=\"NOTIFY_OPS_TO\"]/v)')))),'dgsRegistry@nitda.gov.ng',trim(string(xpath(outputs('Compose_Notify_Config_Xml'),'string(/r/i[k=\"NOTIFY_OPS_TO\"]/v)'))))",
  "replyTo": "@if(empty(trim(string(xpath(outputs('Compose_Notify_Config_Xml'),'string(/r/i[k=\"NOTIFY_REPLY_TO\"]/v)')))),'dgsRegistry@nitda.gov.ng',trim(string(xpath(outputs('Compose_Notify_Config_Xml'),'string(/r/i[k=\"NOTIFY_REPLY_TO\"]/v)'))))",
  "logoSrc": "@if(empty(trim(string(xpath(outputs('Compose_Notify_Config_Xml'),'string(/r/i[k=\"NOTIFY_LOGO_URL\"]/v)')))),outputs('Compose_Notify_Logo_Default'),trim(string(xpath(outputs('Compose_Notify_Config_Xml'),'string(/r/i[k=\"NOTIFY_LOGO_URL\"]/v)'))))"
}
```

### `Scope_Dynamic_Flow/Scope_Notifications/Switch_Notification_Event`

| Field | As provisioned |
|---|---|
| Type | `Switch` |
| Kind | — |
| Description | _not declared_ |
| Runs after | `Compose_Notify_Config` (Succeeded) |

_This action declares no `inputs`._

**`expression`, verbatim:**

```json
"@if(equals(outputs('Compose_Notify_Config')?['enabled'],true),coalesce(actions('Compose_Outcome')?['outputs']?['event'],'none'),'disabled')"
```

### `Scope_Dynamic_Flow/Scope_Notifications/Switch_Notification_Event/case:Case_Server_Error/Select_Failed_Step_Rows`

| Field | As provisioned |
|---|---|
| Type | `Select` |
| Kind | — |
| Description | _not declared_ |
| Runs after | _first in its scope_ |

**`inputs`, verbatim:**

```json
{
  "from": "@result('Scope_Global_Dynamic')",
  "select": "@if(equals(string(item()?['status']),'Failed'),concat('<tr><td valign=\"top\" width=\"38%\" style=\"padding:8px 12px 8px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #F2F4F7;font-family:Cascadia Mono,Consolas,SF Mono,Menlo,Courier New,monospace;font-size:12px;line-height:18px;font-weight:700;color:#1B1A1A;word-break:break-all;\">',replace(replace(replace(replace(replace(string(string(item()?['name'])),'&','&amp;'),'<','&lt;'),'>','&gt;'),'\"','&quot;'),'''','&#39;'),'</td><td valign=\"top\" style=\"padding:8px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #F0EFEF;font-family:Inter,Segoe UI,Roboto,Helvetica Neue,Arial,sans-serif;font-size:12px;line-height:18px;color:#373435;word-break:break-word;\"><span style=\"display:inline-block;font-family:Cascadia Mono,Consolas,SF Mono,Menlo,Courier New,monospace;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#780820;background:#FBE7EB;border-radius:4px;padding:1px 6px;margin-bottom:3px;\">',replace(replace(replace(replace(replace(string(string(coalesce(item()?['error']?['code'],item()?['code'],'Failed'))),'&','&amp;'),'<','&lt;'),'>','&gt;'),'\"','&quot;'),'''','&#39;'),'</span><br>',replace(replace(replace(replace(replace(string(concat(substring(string(coalesce(item()?['error']?['message'],'No message returned.')),0,min(length(string(coalesce(item()?['error']?['message'],'No message returned.'))),120)),if(greater(length(string(coalesce(item()?['error']?['message'],''))),120),' …',''))),'&','&amp;'),'<','&lt;'),'>','&gt;'),'\"','&quot;'),'''','&#39;'),'</td></tr>'),'')"
}
```

```json
  },
  "response_record": {
    "status_code_sent": "@coalesce(actions('Compose_Outcome')?['outputs']?['status'],500)",
    "error_count": "@length(coalesce(actions('Compose_Outcome')?['outputs']?['errors'],json('[]')))"
  },
  "outcome": {
    "flow_outcome": "@if(less(int(coalesce(actions('Compose_Outcome')?['outputs']?['status'],500)),400),'Succeeded','Failed')"
  },
  "timing": {
    "received_at_utc": "@outputs('Compose_Request_Context')?['receivedAtUtc']",
    "completed_at_utc": "@string(coalesce(actions('Compose_Completed_At')?['outputs'],utcNow()))",
    "duration_ms": "@coalesce(actions('Compose_Duration_Ms')?['outputs'],0)"
  },
  "notifications": {
    "event": "@coalesce(actions('Compose_Outcome')?['outputs']?['event'],'none')",
    "scope_status": "@coalesce(actions('Scope_Notifications')?['status'],'Unknown')"
  }
}
```
